===== M0007 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 816-843
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
816 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
817 |     // unknown
818 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
819 |       return true;
820 |     }
821 |     // equality
822 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
823 |       return true;
824 |     }
825 |     // all type
826 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
827 |       return true;
828 |     }
829 |     // unions
830 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
831 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
832 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
833 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
834 |           return true;
835 |         }
836 |       }
837 |     }
838 |     // named types
839 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
840 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).referencedType);
841 |     }
842 |     return false;
843 |   }

150 |   @Override
151 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType that) {
152 |     if (this == that) {
153 |       return true;
154 |     } else if (this.isNominalType()) {
155 |       ObjectType thatObj = ObjectType.cast(that);
156 |       if (thatObj != null && thatObj.isNominalType()) {
157 |         return getReferenceName().equals(thatObj.getReferenceName());
158 |       }
159 |     }
160 |     return false;
161 |   }

/**
   * Create the annotation names from the user-specified
   * annotation whitelist.
   */
Method code:
69 |   private static Map<String, Annotation> buildAnnotationNames(
70 |       Set<String> annotationWhitelist) {
71 |     ImmutableMap.Builder<String, Annotation> annotationBuilder =
72 |         ImmutableMap.builder();
73 |     annotationBuilder.putAll(Annotation.recognizedAnnotations);
74 |     for (String unrecognizedAnnotation : annotationWhitelist) {
75 |       if (!Annotation.recognizedAnnotations.containsKey(
76 |               unrecognizedAnnotation)) {
77 |         annotationBuilder.put(
78 |             unrecognizedAnnotation, Annotation.NOT_IMPLEMENTED);
79 |       }
80 |     }
81 |     return annotationBuilder.build();
82 |   }

===== M0028 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldShift(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 652-713
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to fold shift operations
   */
Method code:
652 |   private Node tryFoldShift(Node n, Node left, Node right) {
653 |     if (left.getType() == Token.NUMBER &&
654 |         right.getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
655 | 
656 |       double result;
657 |       double lval = left.getDouble();
658 |       double rval = right.getDouble();
659 | 
660 |       // check ranges.  We do not do anything that would clip the double to
661 |       // a 32-bit range, since the user likely does not intend that.
662 |       if (!(lval >= Integer.MIN_VALUE && lval <= Integer.MAX_VALUE)) {
663 |         error(BITWISE_OPERAND_OUT_OF_RANGE, left);
664 |         return n;
665 |       }
666 | 
667 |       // only the lower 5 bits are used when shifting, so don't do anything
668 |       // if the shift amount is outside [0,32)
669 |       if (!(rval >= 0 && rval < 32)) {
670 |         error(SHIFT_AMOUNT_OUT_OF_BOUNDS, right);
671 |         return n;
672 |       }
673 | 
674 |       // Convert the numbers to ints
675 |       int lvalInt = (int) lval;
676 |       if (lvalInt != lval) {
677 |         error(FRACTIONAL_BITWISE_OPERAND, left);
678 |         return n;
679 |       }
680 | 
681 |       int rvalInt = (int) rval;
682 |       if (rvalInt != rval) {
683 |         error(FRACTIONAL_BITWISE_OPERAND, right);
684 |         return n;
685 |       }
686 | 
687 |       switch (n.getType()) {
688 |         case Token.LSH:
689 |           result = lvalInt << rvalInt;
690 |           break;
691 |         case Token.RSH:
692 |           result = lvalInt >> rvalInt;
693 |           break;
694 |         case Token.URSH:
695 |           // JavaScript handles zero shifts on signed numbers differently than
696 |           // Java as an Java int can not represent the unsigned 32-bit number
697 |           // where JavaScript can so use a long here.
698 |           result = lvalInt >>> rvalInt;
699 |           break;
700 |         default:
701 |           throw new AssertionError("Unknown shift operator: " +
702 |               Node.tokenToName(n.getType()));
703 |       }
704 | 
705 |       Node newNumber = Node.newNumber(result);
706 |       n.getParent().replaceChild(n, newNumber);
707 |       reportCodeChange();
708 | 
709 |       return newNumber;
710 |     }
711 | 
712 |     return n;
713 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
55 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
56 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
57 |       addGuards(((ComposeWarningsGuard) guard).guards);
58 |     } else {
59 |       int index = Collections.binarySearch(this.guards, guard, guardComparator);
60 |       if (index < 0) {
61 |         index = -index - 1;
62 |       }
63 |       this.guards.add(index, guard);
64 |     }
65 |   }

===== M0055 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 421-447
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
421 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
422 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
423 | 
424 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
425 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
426 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
427 |       return 0;
428 |     }
429 | 
430 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
431 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
432 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
433 |       // a new statistic may have been added
434 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
435 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
436 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
437 |       }
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
441 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
442 |       return 0;
443 |     }
444 | 
445 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
446 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
447 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 369-385
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
369 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
370 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
371 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
372 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
373 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
374 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
375 |         String inputName = input.getName();
376 | 
377 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
378 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
379 |         // will get an error down the line.
380 |         inputs.add(input);
381 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
382 |       }
383 |     }
384 |     return inputs;
385 |   }

1068 |   private static void toStringTreeHelper(Node n, int level, Appendable sb)

52 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
53 |     super(
54 |         registry, name, null,

56 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),

Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 702-723
Documentation:
/**
   * Detaches child from Node and replaces it with newChild.
   */
Method code:
702 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {
703 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
704 |         "The new child node has siblings.");
705 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.parent == null,
706 |         "The new child node already has a parent.");
707 | 
708 |     // Copy over important information.
709 |     newChild.copyInformationFrom(child);
710 | 
711 |     newChild.next = child.next;
712 |     newChild.parent = this;
713 |     if (child == first) {
714 |         first = newChild;
715 |     } else {
716 |         Node prev = getChildBefore(child);
717 |         prev.next = newChild;
718 |     }
719 |     if (child == last)
720 |         last = newChild;
721 |     child.next = null;
722 |     child.parent = null;
723 |   }

931 |   private void toString(
932 |       StringBuilder sb,
933 |       boolean printSource,
934 |       boolean printAnnotations,
935 |       boolean printType) {
936 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
937 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
938 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
939 |         sb.append(' ');
940 |         sb.append(getString());
941 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
942 |         sb.append(' ');
943 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
944 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
945 |         // toStringTree.
946 |         if (first.getType() == Token.STRING) {
947 |           sb.append(first.getString());
948 |         }
949 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
950 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
951 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
952 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
953 |           sb.append(' ');
954 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
955 |         }
956 |         if (printSource) {
957 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
958 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
959 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
960 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
961 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
962 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
963 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
964 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
965 |           sb.append(']');
966 |         }
967 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
968 |         sb.append(' ');
969 |         sb.append(getDouble());
970 |       }
971 |       if (printSource) {
972 |         int lineno = getLineno();
973 |         if (lineno != -1) {
974 |           sb.append(' ');
975 |           sb.append(lineno);
976 |         }
977 |       }
978 | 
979 |       if (printAnnotations) {
980 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
981 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
982 |           int type = keys[i];
983 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
984 |           sb.append(" [");
985 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
986 |           sb.append(": ");
987 |           String value;
988 |           switch (type) {
989 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
990 |               value = "target block property";
991 |               break;
992 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
993 |               value = "last local block";
994 |               break;
995 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
996 |               switch (x.intValue) {
997 |                 case BOTH:
998 |                   value = "both";
999 |                   break;
1000 |                 case RIGHT:
1001 |                   value = "right";
1002 |                   break;
1003 |                 case LEFT:
1004 |                   value = "left";
1005 |                   break;
1006 |                 default:
1007 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1008 |               }
1009 |               break;
1010 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1011 |               switch (x.intValue) {
1012 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1013 |                   value = "eval";
1014 |                   break;
1015 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1016 |                   value = "with";
1017 |                   break;
1018 |                 default:
1019 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1020 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1021 |               }
1022 |               break;
1023 |             default:
1024 |               Object obj = x.objectValue;
1025 |               if (obj != null) {
1026 |                 value = obj.toString();
1027 |               } else {
1028 |                 value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
1029 |               }
1030 |               break;
1031 |           }
1032 |           sb.append(value);
1033 |           sb.append(']');
1034 |         }
1035 |       }
1036 | 
1037 |       if (printType) {
1038 |         if (jsType != null) {
1039 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1040 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1041 |             sb.append(" : ");
1042 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1043 |           }
1044 |         }
1045 |       }
1046 |     }
1047 |   }

===== M0108 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 59-76
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
59 |   @Override
60 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
61 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
62 |       return false;
63 |     }
64 | 
65 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
66 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
67 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
68 |     if (pos >= 0) {
69 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
70 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
71 |         return false;
72 |       }
73 |     }
74 | 
75 |     return isConstantKey(name);
76 |   }

563 |     }
564 |     cfgs.pop();
565 |   }

===== M0126 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
65 |   private String replacePlaceHolders(String s) {
66 |     s = Pattern.quote(s);
67 |     return s.replaceAll("\\{\\d+\\}", "\\\\E.*\\\\Q");
68 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
251 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
252 |     this.type = type;
253 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
254 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
255 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
256 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
257 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
258 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
259 |       int i = 0;
260 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
261 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
262 |         i++;
263 |       }
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
267 | 
268 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
269 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
270 |       return;
271 |     }
272 | 
273 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
274 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
275 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
276 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
277 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
278 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
279 |       trace.truncateEvents();
280 |     }
281 | 
282 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
283 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
284 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
285 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
286 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
287 |                     + "try/finally block. "
288 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
289 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
290 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     trace.startEvent(this);
294 |   }

===== M0131 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1624-1634
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
1624 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
1625 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
1626 |     int len = s.length();
1627 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
1628 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
1629 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
1630 |         return false;
1631 |       }
1632 |     }
1633 |     return true;
1634 |   }

===== M0137 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 783-797
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
783 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
784 |     if (item == null) {
785 |       return null;
786 |     } else if (item.type == propType) {
787 |       return item.next;
788 |     } else {
789 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.next, propType);
790 |       if (result != item.next) {
791 |         return new PropListItem(
792 |             item.type, item.intValue, item.objectValue, result);
793 |       } else {
794 |         return item;
795 |       }
796 |     }
797 |   }

===== M0156 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 87-142
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
87 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
88 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
89 |     // just bail out early here.
90 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
91 |       return this;
92 |     }
93 | 
94 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
95 | 
96 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
97 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
98 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
99 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
100 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
101 |     }
102 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
103 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
104 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
105 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
106 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
107 |         }
108 |       } else {
109 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
110 |           return this;
111 |         }
112 | 
113 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
114 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
115 |         // one another.
116 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
117 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
118 |           JSType current = it.next();
119 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
120 |               current.isUnknownType()) {
121 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
122 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
123 |               return this;
124 |             }
125 |           } else {
126 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
127 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
128 |               return this;
129 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
130 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
131 |               it.remove();
132 |             }
133 |           }
134 |         }
135 |         alternates.add(alternate);
136 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
137 |       }
138 |     } else {
139 |       result = null;
140 |     }
141 |     return this;
142 |   }

===== M0164 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1603-1640
Documentation:
/** Returns true if this node is equivalent semantically to another */
Method code:
1603 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node) {
1604 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1605 |       try {
1606 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1607 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1608 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1609 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1610 |             return false;
1611 |           }
1612 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1613 |           return false;
1614 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1615 |           return false;
1616 |         } else {
1617 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1618 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1619 |               return false;
1620 |             }
1621 |           }
1622 |         }
1623 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1624 |         return false;
1625 |       }
1626 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1627 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1628 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1629 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1630 |         return false;
1631 |       }
1632 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1633 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1634 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1635 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1636 |         return false;
1637 |       }
1638 |     }
1639 |     return true;
1640 |   }

63 |   @Override
64 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
65 |     Node currentVersionOfNode = n;
66 |     
67 |     boolean somethingChanged = false;
68 |     
69 |     do {
70 |       somethingChanged = false;
71 |       for (AbstractPeepholeOptimization optimization : peepholeOptimizations) {

73 |             optimization.optimizeSubtree(currentVersionOfNode);
74 |         
75 |         if (newVersionOfNode != currentVersionOfNode) {
76 |           somethingChanged = true;
77 |

79 |         }
80 |         
81 |         if (currentVersionOfNode == null) {
82 |           return;
83 |         }
84 |       }
85 |     } while(somethingChanged);

===== M0172 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldAssign(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 328-393
Documentation:

Method code:
328 |   private Node tryFoldAssign(Node n, Node left, Node right) {
329 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.getType() == Token.ASSIGN);
330 | 
331 |     // Tries to convert x = x + y -> x += y;
332 |     if (!right.hasChildren() ||
333 |         right.getFirstChild().getNext() != right.getLastChild()) {
334 |       // RHS must have two children.
335 |       return n;
336 |     }
337 | 
338 |     if (NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(left)) {
339 |       return n;
340 |     }
341 | 
342 |     Node leftChild = right.getFirstChild();
343 |     if (!areNodesEqualForInlining(left, leftChild)) {
344 |       return n;
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     int newType = -1;
348 |     switch (right.getType()) {
349 |       case Token.ADD:
350 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_ADD;
351 |         break;
352 |       case Token.BITAND:
353 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITAND;
354 |         break;
355 |       case Token.BITOR:
356 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITOR;
357 |         break;
358 |       case Token.BITXOR:
359 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR;
360 |         break;
361 |       case Token.DIV:
362 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_DIV;
363 |         break;
364 |       case Token.LSH:
365 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_LSH;
366 |         break;
367 |       case Token.MOD:
368 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_MOD;
369 |         break;
370 |       case Token.MUL:
371 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_MUL;
372 |         break;
373 |       case Token.RSH:
374 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_RSH;
375 |         break;
376 |       case Token.SUB:
377 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_SUB;
378 |         break;
379 |       case Token.URSH:
380 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_URSH;
381 |         break;
382 |       default:
383 |         return n;
384 |     }
385 | 
386 |     Node newNode = new Node(newType,
387 |         left.detachFromParent(), right.getLastChild().detachFromParent());
388 |     n.getParent().replaceChild(n, newNode);
389 | 
390 |     reportCodeChange();
391 | 
392 |     return newNode;
393 |   }

86 |       String sourceStr) {

Method code:
288 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
289 |     if (prototype == null) {
290 |       return false;
291 |     }
292 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
293 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
294 |       return false;
295 |     }

298 |

301 |       if (superClass != null) {

303 |       }
304 |     }
305 |     return true;
306 |   }

===== M0210 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 997-1129
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
997 |   Node parseInputs() {
998 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
999 | 
1000 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1001 |     // individual file parse trees.
1002 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1003 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1004 |     }
1005 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1006 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1007 |     }
1008 | 
1009 |     // Parse main js sources.
1010 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1011 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1012 | 
1013 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1014 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1015 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1016 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1017 |     }
1018 | 
1019 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1020 | 
1021 |     try {
1022 |       // Parse externs sources.
1023 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1024 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1025 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1026 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1027 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1028 |           return null;
1029 |         }
1030 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1031 |       }
1032 | 
1033 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1034 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1035 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1036 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1037 | 
1038 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1039 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1040 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1041 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1042 |           }
1043 |         }
1044 | 
1045 |         try {
1046 |           inputs =
1047 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1048 |               .manageDependencies(inputs);
1049 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1050 |           report(JSError.make(
1051 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1052 |           return null;
1053 |         }
1054 |       }
1055 | 
1056 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1057 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1058 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1059 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1060 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1061 |           return null;
1062 |         }
1063 | 
1064 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1065 |         if (n == null) {
1066 |           continue;
1067 |         }
1068 | 
1069 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1070 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1071 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1072 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1073 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1074 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1075 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1076 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1077 | 
1078 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1079 | 
1080 |             externs.add(input);
1081 |             staleInputs = true;
1082 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1083 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1084 |             staleInputs = true;
1085 |           }
1086 |         }
1087 |       }
1088 | 
1089 |       if (staleInputs) {
1090 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1091 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1092 |       }
1093 | 
1094 |       // Build the AST.
1095 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1096 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1097 |         if (n == null) {
1098 |           continue;
1099 |         }
1100 | 
1101 |         if (devMode) {
1102 |           runSanityCheck();
1103 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1104 |             return null;
1105 |           }
1106 |         }
1107 | 
1108 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1109 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1110 | 
1111 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1112 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1113 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1114 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1115 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1116 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1117 |         }
1118 | 
1119 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1120 |       }
1121 | 
1122 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1123 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1124 | 
1125 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1126 |     } finally {
1127 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1128 |     }
1129 |   }

===== M0211 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 206-234
Documentation:

Method code:
206 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
207 |     maybeEndStatement();
208 | 
209 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
210 |     char prev = getLastChar();
211 | 
212 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
213 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
214 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
215 |       append(" ");
216 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
217 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
218 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
221 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
222 |       append(" ");
223 |     }
224 | 
225 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
226 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
227 | 
228 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
229 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
230 |     // because it looks strange.
231 |     if (binOp) {
232 |       maybeCutLine();
233 |     }
234 |   }

/**

   */
Method code:
60 |   protected void reportCodeChange() {

===== M0213 =====

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 578-601
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a tree to js code
   */
Method code:
578 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
579 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  int lineLengthThreshold,
580 |                                  SourceMap sourceMap,
581 |                                  Charset outputCharset) {
582 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
583 |     MappedCodePrinter mcp =
584 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
585 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
586 |             lineBreak, lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap)
587 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap);
588 |     CodeGenerator cg =
589 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
590 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset)
591 |         : new CodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset);
592 |     cg.add(root);
593 | 
594 |     String code = mcp.getCode();
595 | 
596 |     if (createSourceMap) {
597 |       mcp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
598 |     }
599 | 
600 |     return code;
601 |   }

===== M0231 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 406-450
Documentation:

Method code:
406 |   @Override
407 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
408 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
409 |       return true;
410 |     }
411 | 
412 |     // Union types
413 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
414 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
415 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
416 |       return false;
417 |     }
418 | 
419 |     // record types
420 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
421 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
422 |     }
423 | 
424 |     // Interfaces
425 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
426 |     // to the interface instance.
427 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
428 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
429 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
430 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
431 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
432 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
433 |           return true;
434 |         }
435 |       }
436 |     }
437 | 
438 |     // other prototype based objects
439 |     if (that != null) {
440 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
441 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
442 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
443 |         // to avoid guessing.
444 |         return true;
445 |       }
446 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     return false;
450 |   }

===== M0259 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1092-1103
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1092 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1093 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1094 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1095 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1096 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1097 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1098 |       } else {
1099 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1100 |       }
1101 |     }
1102 |     return builder.build();
1103 |   }

===== M0280 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldBinaryOperator(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 88-149
Documentation:

Method code:
88 |   private Node tryFoldBinaryOperator(Node subtree) {
89 |     Node left = subtree.getFirstChild();
90 | 
91 |     if (left == null) {
92 |       return subtree;
93 |     }
94 | 
95 |     Node right = left.getNext();
96 | 
97 |     if (right == null) {
98 |       return subtree;
99 |     }
100 | 
101 |     // If we've reached here, node is truly a binary operator.
102 |     switch(subtree.getType()) {
103 |       case Token.GETPROP:
104 |         return tryFoldGetProp(subtree, left, right);
105 | 
106 |       case Token.GETELEM:
107 |         return tryFoldGetElem(subtree, left, right);
108 | 
109 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
110 |         return tryFoldInstanceof(subtree, left, right);
111 | 
112 |       case Token.AND:
113 |       case Token.OR:
114 |         return tryFoldAndOr(subtree, left, right);
115 | 
116 |       case Token.BITAND:
117 |       case Token.BITOR:
118 |         return tryFoldBitAndOr(subtree, left, right);
119 | 
120 |       case Token.LSH:
121 |       case Token.RSH:
122 |       case Token.URSH:
123 |         return tryFoldShift(subtree, left, right);
124 | 
125 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
126 |         return tryFoldAssign(subtree, left, right);
127 | 
128 |       case Token.ADD:
129 |         return tryFoldAdd(subtree, left, right);
130 | 
131 |       case Token.SUB:
132 |       case Token.MUL:
133 |       case Token.DIV:
134 |         return tryFoldArithmetic(subtree, left, right);
135 | 
136 |       case Token.LT:
137 |       case Token.GT:
138 |       case Token.LE:
139 |       case Token.GE:
140 |       case Token.EQ:
141 |       case Token.NE:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |         return tryFoldComparison(subtree, left, right);
145 | 
146 |       default:
147 |         return subtree;
148 |     }
149 |   }

===== M0292 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1412-1435
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1412 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1413 |     boolean eq = false;
1414 | 
1415 |     if (type == node2.getType() && getChildCount() == node2.getChildCount()
1416 |         && getNodeClass(this) == getNodeClass(node2)) {
1417 |       eq = this.isEquivalentTo(node2);
1418 |     }
1419 | 
1420 |     if (!eq) {
1421 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1422 |     }
1423 | 
1424 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1425 |     Node n, n2;
1426 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1427 |          res == null && n != null;
1428 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1429 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1430 |       if (res != null) {
1431 |         return res;
1432 |       }
1433 |     }
1434 |     return res;
1435 |   }

   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since

   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.

===== M0307 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 401-415
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
401 |   @Override
402 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
403 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
404 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
405 |     if (unknown) {
406 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
407 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
408 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
409 |         unknown = false;
410 |       } else {
411 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
412 |       }
413 |     }
414 |     return unknown;
415 |   }

===== M0308 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 80-114
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
80 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
81 |                            String sourceString,
82 |                            Config config,
83 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
84 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
85 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
86 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
87 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
88 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
89 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
90 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
91 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
92 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
93 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
94 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
95 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
99 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
100 |     try {
101 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
102 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
103 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
104 |     } finally {
105 |       Context.exit();
106 |     }
107 |     Node root = null;
108 |     if (astRoot != null) {
109 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
110 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
111 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
112 |     }
113 |     return root;
114 |   }

===== M0310 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 236-262
Documentation:

Method code:
236 |   void addNumber(double x) {
237 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
238 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
239 |     char prev = getLastChar();
240 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
241 |       add(" ");
242 |     }
243 | 
244 |     if ((long) x == x) {
245 |       long value = (long) x;
246 |       long mantissa = value;
247 |       int exp = 0;
248 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
249 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
250 |           mantissa /= 10;
251 |           exp++;
252 |         }
253 |       }
254 |       if (exp > 2) {
255 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
256 |       } else {
257 |         add(Long.toString(value));
258 |       }
259 |     } else {
260 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
261 |     }
262 |   }

===== M0318 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 383-398
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
383 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
384 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
385 |       Set<String> directives = null;
386 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
387 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
388 |         if (directives == null) {
389 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
390 |         } else {
391 |           directives.add(directive);
392 |         }
393 |       }
394 | 
395 |       if (directives != null) {
396 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
397 |       }
398 |     }

===== M0329 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 659-734
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
659 |   static int precedence(int type) {
660 |     switch (type) {
661 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
662 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
663 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
664 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
665 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
666 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
667 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
668 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
669 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
670 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
671 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
672 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
673 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
674 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
675 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
676 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
677 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
678 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
679 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
680 |       case Token.EQ:
681 |       case Token.NE:
682 |       case Token.SHEQ:
683 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
684 |       case Token.LT:
685 |       case Token.GT:
686 |       case Token.LE:
687 |       case Token.GE:
688 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
689 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
690 |       case Token.LSH:
691 |       case Token.RSH:
692 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
693 |       case Token.SUB:
694 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
695 |       case Token.MUL:
696 |       case Token.MOD:
697 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
698 |       case Token.INC:
699 |       case Token.DEC:
700 |       case Token.NEW:
701 |       case Token.DELPROP:
702 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
703 |       case Token.VOID:
704 |       case Token.NOT:
705 |       case Token.BITNOT:
706 |       case Token.POS:
707 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
708 | 
709 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
710 |       case Token.CALL:
711 |       case Token.EMPTY:
712 |       case Token.FALSE:
713 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
714 |       case Token.GETELEM:
715 |       case Token.GETPROP:
716 |       case Token.GET_REF:
717 |       case Token.IF:
718 |       case Token.LP:
719 |       case Token.NAME:
720 |       case Token.NULL:
721 |       case Token.NUMBER:
722 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
723 |       case Token.REGEXP:
724 |       case Token.RETURN:
725 |       case Token.STRING:
726 |       case Token.THIS:
727 |       case Token.TRUE:
728 |         return 15;
729 | 
730 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
731 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
732 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
733 |     }
734 |   }

===== M0330 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 951-971
Documentation:

Method code:
951 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
952 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
953 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
954 |       return s;
955 |     }
956 | 
957 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
958 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
959 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
960 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
961 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
962 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
963 |       // crazy.
964 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
965 |         sb.append(c);
966 |       } else {
967 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
968 |       }
969 |     }
970 |     return sb.toString();
971 |   }

===== M0331 =====

Method code:
55 |   @Override
56 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
57 |     if (checkOnly) {
58 |       normalizeNodeTypes(root);
59 |     } else {
60 |       // Don't perform "PrepareAnnoations" when doing checks as
61 |       // they currently aren't valid during sanity checks.  In particular,
62 |       // they DIRECT_EVAL shouldn't be applied after inlining has been
63 |       // performed.
64 |       if (externs != null) {
65 |         NodeTraversal.traverse(
66 |             compiler, externs, new PrepareAnnotations(compiler));
67 |       }
68 |       if (root != null) {
69 |         NodeTraversal.traverse(
70 |             compiler, root, new PrepareAnnotations(compiler));
71 |       }
72 |     }
73 |   }

/**
   * If we're in a IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE, we can't permit in operators in the
   * expression.  Pass on the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE flag through subexpressions.

===== M0338 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 76-648
Documentation:

Method code:
76 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
77 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
78 |       return;
79 |     }
80 | 
81 |     int type = n.getType();
82 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
83 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
84 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
85 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
86 | 
87 |     // Handle all binary operators
88 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
89 |       Preconditions.checkState(
90 |           childCount == 2,
91 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
92 |           opstr, childCount);
93 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
94 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
95 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
96 | 
97 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
98 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
99 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
100 | 
101 |       // Handle associativity.
102 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
103 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
104 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
105 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
106 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
107 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
108 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
109 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
110 |       } else {
111 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
112 |       }
113 |       return;
114 |     }
115 | 
116 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
117 | 
118 |     switch (type) {
119 |       case Token.TRY: {
120 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
121 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
122 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
123 | 
124 |         add("try");
125 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
126 | 
127 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
128 |         // isn't a catch block
129 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
130 |         if (catchblock != null) {
131 |           add(catchblock);
132 |         }
133 | 
134 |         if (childCount == 3) {
135 |           add("finally");
136 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
137 |         }
138 |         break;
139 |       }
140 | 
141 |       case Token.CATCH:
142 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
143 |         if (first.getNext().getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
144 |           throw new Error("Catch conditions not suppored because I think" +
145 |                           " that it may be a netscape only feature.");
146 |         }
147 | 
148 |         add("catch(");
149 |         add(first);
150 |         add(")");
151 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
152 |         break;
153 | 
154 |       case Token.THROW:
155 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
156 |         add("throw");
157 |         add(first);
158 | 
159 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
160 |         // parse this.
161 |         cc.endStatement(true);
162 |         break;
163 | 
164 |       case Token.RETURN:
165 |         add("return");
166 |         if (childCount == 1) {
167 |           add(first);
168 |         } else {
169 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
170 |         }
171 |         cc.endStatement();
172 |         break;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.VAR:
175 |         if (first != null) {
176 |           add("var ");
177 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
178 |         }
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
182 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
183 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
184 |         break;
185 | 
186 |       case Token.NAME:
187 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
188 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
189 |         } else {
190 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
191 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
192 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
193 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
194 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
195 |           } else {
196 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
197 |             // precedence.
198 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
199 |           }
200 |         }
201 |         break;
202 | 
203 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
204 |         add("[");
205 |         addList(first, (int[]) n.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP));
206 |         add("]");
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.LP:
210 |         add("(");
211 |         addList(first);
212 |         add(")");
213 |         break;
214 | 
215 |       case Token.COMMA:
216 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
217 |         addList(first, false, context);
218 |         break;
219 | 
220 |       case Token.NUMBER:
221 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
222 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
223 |         break;
224 | 
225 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
226 |       case Token.VOID:
227 |       case Token.NOT:
228 |       case Token.BITNOT:
229 |       case Token.POS:
230 |       case Token.NEG: {
231 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
232 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
233 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
234 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
235 |         break;
236 |       }
237 | 
238 |       case Token.HOOK: {
239 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
240 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
241 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
242 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
243 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
244 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
245 |         addExpr(last, 1);
246 |         break;
247 |       }
248 | 
249 |       case Token.REGEXP:
250 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
251 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
252 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
253 |         }
254 | 
255 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
256 | 
257 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
258 |         if (childCount == 2) {
259 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
260 |         } else {
261 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
262 |           add(regexp);
263 |         }
264 |         break;
265 | 
266 |       case Token.GET_REF:
267 |         add(first);
268 |         break;
269 | 
270 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
271 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
272 |         add(first);
273 |         add(".");
274 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
275 |         break;
276 | 
277 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
278 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
279 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
280 |         }
281 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
282 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
283 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
284 |           add("(");
285 |         }
286 | 
287 |         add("function");
288 |         add(first);
289 | 
290 |         add(first.getNext());
291 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
292 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
293 | 
294 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
295 |           add(")");
296 |         }
297 |         break;
298 | 
299 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
300 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
301 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
302 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
303 |         }
304 |         boolean stripBlock = n.isSyntheticBlock() ||
305 |             ((context != Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK) && (n.getChildCount() < 2));
306 |         if (!stripBlock) {
307 |           cc.beginBlock();
308 |         }
309 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
310 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
311 | 
312 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
313 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
314 |             cc.endStatement();
315 |           }
316 | 
317 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
318 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
319 |           }
320 | 
321 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
322 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
323 |           if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
324 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
325 |           }
326 |         }
327 |         if (!stripBlock) {
328 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
329 |         }
330 |         break;
331 |       }
332 | 
333 |       case Token.FOR:
334 |         if (childCount == 4) {
335 |           add("for(");
336 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
337 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
338 |           } else {
339 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
340 |           }
341 |           add(";");
342 |           add(first.getNext());
343 |           add(";");
344 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
345 |           add(")");
346 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
347 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
348 |         } else {
349 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
350 |           add("for(");
351 |           add(first);
352 |           add("in");
353 |           add(first.getNext());
354 |           add(")");
355 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
356 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
357 |         }
358 |         break;
359 | 
360 |       case Token.DO:
361 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
362 |         add("do");
363 |         addNonEmptyExpression(first, Context.OTHER, false);
364 |         add("while(");
365 |         add(last);
366 |         add(")");
367 |         cc.endStatement();
368 |         break;
369 | 
370 |       case Token.WHILE:
371 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
372 |         add("while(");
373 |         add(first);
374 |         add(")");
375 |         addNonEmptyExpression(
376 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
377 |         break;
378 | 
379 |       case Token.EMPTY:
380 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
381 |         break;
382 | 
383 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
384 |         Preconditions.checkState(
385 |             childCount == 2,
386 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
387 |         Preconditions.checkState(
388 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
389 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
390 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
391 |         if (needsParens) {
392 |           add("(");
393 |         }
394 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
395 |         if (needsParens) {
396 |           add(")");
397 |         }
398 |         add(".");
399 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
400 |         break;
401 |       }
402 | 
403 |       case Token.GETELEM:
404 |         Preconditions.checkState(
405 |             childCount == 2,
406 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
407 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
408 |         add("[");
409 |         add(first.getNext());
410 |         add("]");
411 |         break;
412 | 
413 |       case Token.WITH:
414 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
415 |         add("with(");
416 |         add(first);
417 |         add(")");
418 |         addNonEmptyExpression(
419 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
420 |         break;
421 | 
422 |       case Token.INC:
423 |       case Token.DEC: {
424 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
425 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
426 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
427 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
428 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
429 |         if (postProp != 0) {
430 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
431 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
432 |         } else {
433 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
434 |           add(first);
435 |         }
436 |         break;
437 |       }
438 | 
439 |       case Token.CALL:
440 |         // We have two special cases here:
441 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
442 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
443 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
444 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
445 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
446 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
447 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
448 |         // that must be preserved.
449 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
450 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
451 |           add("(0,");
452 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
453 |           add(")");
454 |         } else {
455 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
456 |         }
457 |         add("(");
458 |         addList(first.getNext());
459 |         add(")");
460 |         break;
461 | 
462 |       case Token.IF:
463 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
464 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
465 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
466 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
467 |           cc.beginBlock();
468 |         }
469 | 
470 |         add("if(");
471 |         add(first);
472 |         add(")");
473 | 
474 |         if (hasElse) {
475 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
476 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
477 |           add("else");
478 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
479 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
480 |         } else {
481 |           addNonEmptyExpression(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
482 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
483 |         }
484 | 
485 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
486 |           cc.endBlock();
487 |         }
488 |         break;
489 | 
490 |       case Token.NULL:
491 |       case Token.THIS:
492 |       case Token.FALSE:
493 |       case Token.TRUE:
494 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
495 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
496 |         break;
497 | 
498 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
499 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
500 |         add("continue");
501 |         if (childCount == 1) {
502 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
503 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
504 |           }
505 |           add(" ");
506 |           add(first);
507 |         }
508 |         cc.endStatement();
509 |         break;
510 | 
511 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
512 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
513 |         add("debugger");
514 |         cc.endStatement();
515 |         break;
516 | 
517 |       case Token.BREAK:
518 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
519 |         add("break");
520 |         if (childCount == 1) {
521 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
522 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
523 |           }
524 |           add(" ");
525 |           add(first);
526 |         }
527 |         cc.endStatement();
528 |         break;
529 | 
530 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
531 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
532 | 
533 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
534 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
535 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
536 |         cc.endStatement();
537 |         break;
538 | 
539 |       case Token.NEW:
540 |         add("new ");
541 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
542 | 
543 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
544 |         // to force parens. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
545 |         // first viable parens
546 |         if (NodeUtil.containsCall(first)) {
547 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
548 |         }
549 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
550 | 
551 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
552 |         Node next = first.getNext();
553 |         if (next != null) {
554 |           add("(");
555 |           addList(next);
556 |           add(")");
557 |         }
558 |         break;
559 | 
560 |       case Token.STRING:
561 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
562 |         add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
563 |         break;
564 | 
565 |       case Token.DELPROP:
566 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
567 |         add("delete ");
568 |         add(first);
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
572 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount % 2 == 0);
573 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
574 |         if (needsParens) {
575 |           add("(");
576 |         }
577 |         add("{");
578 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext().getNext()) {
579 |           if (c != first) {
580 |             cc.listSeparator();
581 |           }
582 | 
583 |           // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they are
584 |           // not JavaScript keywords
585 |           if (c.getType() == Token.STRING &&
586 |               !TokenStream.isKeyword(c.getString()) &&
587 |               TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(c.getString()) &&
588 |               // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
589 |               // unicode escaped.
590 |               NodeUtil.isLatin(c.getString())) {
591 |             add(c.getString());
592 |           } else {
593 |             addExpr(c, 1);
594 |           }
595 |           add(":");
596 |           addExpr(c.getNext(), 1);
597 |         }
598 |         add("}");
599 |         if (needsParens) {
600 |           add(")");
601 |         }
602 |         break;
603 |       }
604 | 
605 |       case Token.SWITCH:
606 |         add("switch(");
607 |         add(first);
608 |         add(")");
609 |         cc.beginBlock();
610 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
611 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
612 |         break;
613 | 
614 |       case Token.CASE:
615 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
616 |         add("case ");
617 |         add(first);
618 |         addCaseBody(last);
619 |         break;
620 | 
621 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
622 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
623 |         add("default");
624 |         addCaseBody(first);
625 |         break;
626 | 
627 |       case Token.LABEL:
628 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
629 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
630 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
631 |         }
632 |         add(first);
633 |         add(":");
634 |         addNonEmptyExpression(
635 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
636 |         break;
637 | 
638 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
639 |       // ignored for our purposes.
640 |       case Token.SETNAME:
641 |         break;
642 | 
643 |       default:
644 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
645 |     }
646 | 
647 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
648 |   }

===== M0340 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 441-478
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
441 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
442 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
443 |     int type = n.getType();
444 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
445 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
446 |     }
447 | 
448 |     curNode = n;
449 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
450 | 
451 |     switch (type) {
452 |       case Token.CATCH:
453 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
454 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
455 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
456 |         // is the code block
457 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild(), n);
458 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext(), n);
459 |         break;
460 | 
461 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
462 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
463 |         break;
464 | 
465 |       default:
466 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
467 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
468 |           // would no longer point to the true next
469 |           Node next = child.getNext();
470 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
471 |           child = next;
472 |         }
473 |         break;
474 |     }
475 | 
476 |     curNode = n;
477 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
478 |   }

452 |         createUnionType(OBJECT_TYPE, NUMBER_TYPE, STRING_TYPE, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
453 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN,
454 |         OBJECT_NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN);
455 | 
456 |     // (string,number,boolean)
457 |     JSType NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN =
458 |         createUnionType(NUMBER_TYPE, STRING_TYPE, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
459 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN,
460 |         NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN);
461 | 
462 |     // (string,number)
463 |     JSType NUMBER_STRING = createUnionType(NUMBER_TYPE, STRING_TYPE);
464 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.NUMBER_STRING, NUMBER_STRING);
465 | 
466 |     // Native object properties are filled in by externs...
467 | 
468 |     // (String, string)
469 |     JSType STRING_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE =
470 |         createUnionType(STRING_OBJECT_TYPE, STRING_TYPE);
471 |     registerNativeType(
472 |         JSTypeNative.STRING_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE, STRING_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE);
473 | 
474 |     // (Number, number)
475 |     JSType NUMBER_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE =
476 |         createUnionType(NUMBER_OBJECT_TYPE, NUMBER_TYPE);
477 |     registerNativeType(
478 |         JSTypeNative.NUMBER_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE, NUMBER_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE);
479 | 
480 |     // unknown function type, i.e. (?...) -> ?
481 |     FunctionType U2U_FUNCTION_TYPE =
482 |         createFunctionType(UNKNOWN_TYPE, true, UNKNOWN_TYPE);
483 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.U2U_FUNCTION_TYPE, U2U_FUNCTION_TYPE);
484 | 
485 |     // unknown constructor type, i.e. (?...) -> ? with the NoObject type
486 |     // as instance type

488 |         // This is equivalent to
489 |         // createConstructorType(UNKNOWN_TYPE, true, UNKNOWN_TYPE), but,
490 |         // in addition, overrides getInstanceType() to return the NoObject type
491 |         // instead of a new anonymous object.
492 |         new FunctionType(this, "Function", null,
493 |             createArrowType(
494 |                 createParametersWithVarArgs(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
495 |                 UNKNOWN_TYPE),
496 |             NO_OBJECT_TYPE, null, true, true) {
497 |           private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;
498 | 
499 |           @Override public FunctionType getConstructor() {
500 |             return registry.getNativeFunctionType(
501 |                 JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
502 |           }
503 |         };
504 | 
505 |     // The U2U_CONSTRUCTOR is weird, because it's the supertype of its
506 |     // own constructor.
507 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE, U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
508 |     registerNativeType(
509 |         JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE, U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
510 | 
511 |     FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE.setInstanceType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
512 |     U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE.setImplicitPrototype(FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE);
513 | 
514 |     // least function type, i.e. (All...) -> NoType
515 |     FunctionType LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE =
516 |         createFunctionType(NO_TYPE, true, ALL_TYPE);
517 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE, LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE);
518 | 
519 |     // the 'this' object in the global scope
520 |     ObjectType GLOBAL_THIS = createObjectType("global this", null,
521 |         UNKNOWN_TYPE /* to be resolved later */);

448 |     @Override
449 |     void maybeCutLine() {
450 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
451 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
452 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
453 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
454 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;

===== M0390 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1154-1162
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1154 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1155 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1156 |       return -1;
1157 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1158 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1159 |     } else {
1160 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1161 |     }
1162 |   }
