===== M0392 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 587-612
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
587 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
588 |     ObjectType owner = null;
589 | 
590 |     // Properties can only be defined on object types, so normalize everything
591 |     // to discrete object types.
592 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType) {
593 |       owner = (ObjectType) type;
594 |     } else if (getNativeType(ALL_TYPE).isSubtype(type)) {
595 |       owner = getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE);
596 |     } else if (type instanceof UnionType) {
597 |       for (JSType alternate : ((UnionType) type).getAlternates()) {
598 |         registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, alternate);
599 |       }
600 |     }
601 | 
602 |     if (owner == null) {
603 |       return;
604 |     }
605 | 
606 |     Set<ObjectType> typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
607 |     if (typeSet == null) {
608 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet = Sets.newHashSet());
609 |     }
610 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
611 |     typeSet.add(owner);
612 |   }

===== M0394 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformTree(AstRoot,String,Config,ErrorReporter)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 139-185
Documentation:

Method code:
139 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
140 |                                    String sourceString,
141 |                                    Config config,
142 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
143 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, node.getSourceName(),
144 |         config, errorReporter);
145 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
146 |     // @license text gets appended onto the fileLevelJsDocBuilder as found,
147 |     // and stored straight into the JSDocInfo for the root node.
148 |     Node.FileLevelJsDocBuilder fileLevelJsDocBuilder =
149 |         irNode.getJsDocBuilderForNode();
150 |     // fileOverviewInfo stores the last bit of fileoverview data we saw.
151 |     // We only permit one, so throwing away extras is fair.
152 |     // The fileOverviewInfo gets passed into parseJSDocInfo so that
153 |     // it can detect when multiple @fileoverviews exist in the same file.
154 |     JSDocInfo fileOverviewInfo = null;
155 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
156 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
157 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == JSDOC) {
158 |           JsDocInfoParser jsDocParser =
159 |               irFactory.createJsDocInfoParser(comment.getValue(),
160 |                   comment.getLineno(), comment.getAbsolutePosition(),
161 |                   fileLevelJsDocBuilder, fileOverviewInfo);
162 |           if (jsDocParser.getFileOverviewJSDocInfo() != fileOverviewInfo) {
163 |             fileOverviewInfo = jsDocParser.getFileOverviewJSDocInfo();
164 |           } else {
165 |             JSDocInfo info = jsDocParser.retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
166 |             if (info != null) {
167 |               irFactory.attachJsDoc(comment, info);
168 |             }
169 |           }
170 |         }
171 |       }
172 | 
173 |       // Only after we've seen all @fileoverview entries, attach the
174 |       // last one to the root node, and copy the found license strings
175 |       // to that node.
176 |       if (fileOverviewInfo != null) {
177 |         if ((irNode.getJSDocInfo() != null) &&
178 |             (irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null)) {
179 |           fileOverviewInfo.setLicense(irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense());
180 |         }
181 |         irNode.setJSDocInfo(fileOverviewInfo);
182 |       }
183 |     }
184 |     return irNode;
185 |   }

===== M0397 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 203-233

Method code:
203 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
204 |     String jsDoc = node.getJsDoc();
205 |     NodeWithJsDoc nodeWithJsDoc = null;

214 |     }
215 | 
216 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
217 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
218 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
219 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
220 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
221 |     } else {
222 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
223 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
224 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
225 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
226 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
227 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
228 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
229 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
230 |       }
231 |     }
232 |     return irNode;
233 |   }

/**
   * Determines whether a node represents an object literal key
   * (e.g. key1 in {key1: value1, key2: value2}).
   *
   * @param node A node
   * @param parent The node's parent
   */
Method code:
1332 |   static boolean isObjectLitKey(Node node, Node parent) {
1333 |     if (node.getType() == Token.STRING && parent.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
1334 |       int index = 0;
1335 |       for (Node current = parent.getFirstChild();
1336 |            current != null;
1337 |            current = current.getNext()) {
1338 |         if (current == node) {
1339 |           return index % 2 == 0;
1340 |         }
1341 |         index++;
1342 |       }
1343 |     }
1344 |     return false;
1345 |   }
